                                                ANNUAL REPORT 2018 年報
Corporate
Profile 公 司 概 況
China Foods Limited (“China Foods” or the “Company” and together with its
subsidiaries, the “Group”), a subsidiary of COFCO Corporation (“COFCO”) and the
only focused beverage platform of COFCO, is listed on the main board of The Stock
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Stock Exchange”) (Stock Code: 506).

The Company mainly manufactures, distributes, markets and sells the Coca-Cola
series products at 19 provincial-level administrative regions in the PRC through
COFCO Coca-Cola Beverages Limited (“CCBL”), a 65%-owned joint-venture with The
Coca-Cola Company. CCBL offers eighteen brands of products in nine major types of
beverages which include sparkling drink, juice, milk drink, water, enhanced water,
coffee, ready-to-drink tea, energy drink and plant-based protein drink.

中國食品有限公司（簡稱「中國食品」或「本公司」，連同其附屬公司統稱「本集團」）
為中糧集團有限公司（「中糧」）的附屬公司及中糧唯一的專業化飲料業務平台，並
於香港聯合交易所有限公司（簡稱「聯交所」）主板上市（股份代號：506）。

本公司目前主要通過與可口可樂公司合作成立並持有 65% 權益的中糧可口可樂飲
料有限公司（簡稱「中可飲料」
             ）在國內 19 個省級行政區域從事生產、配送、推廣和
銷售可口可樂系列產品，向消費者提供包括汽水、果汁、果奶、水、功能水、咖
啡、即飲茶、能量飲料及植物蛋白飲料等 9 大品類、18 個品牌的產品。

    中國食品 2018 年大事記


    40TH ANNIVERSARY OF THE COCA-COLA COMPANY’S
    RETURN TO CHINA WITH THE SUPPORT OF COFCO
    在中糧的協助下，可口可樂公司重返中國 40 年紀念

    On 13 December 1978, COFCO Corporation
    (“COFCO”) entered into an agreement with The
    Coca-Cola Company at the Beijing Hotel, which, led
    to its establishment of bottling plants in China and
    distribution of Coca-Cola to major cities and tourist
    areas of China.
    At the same time the agreement was being negotiated,
    the PRC and the United States were negotiating their
    own historic agreement to restore diplomatic relations
    at another meeting room on the same floor of the
    Beijing Hotel. On 16 December 1978, they issued
    a “Joint Communiqué on the Establishment of Diplomatic Relations”, announcing “From 1 January 1979, the two
    governments will establish diplomatic relations at ambassadorial level.”
    1978 年 12 月 13 日，中糧集團有限公司（「中糧」
                                 ）與可口可樂公司在北京飯店簽訂協議，向中國內地的主要城市和旅
    遊區提供可口可樂，並在內地設廠灌裝銷售。
    就在可口可樂與中糧商定合作協議的同時，北京飯店同一層樓另一間會議室里，中美兩國正進行恢復邦交的談判。
    1978 年 12 月 16 日中美雙方發表《中美建交聯合公報》，宣佈「自 1979 年 1 月 1 日起，建立大使級外交關係」。




                                                                      In January 1979, COFCO transported 3,000
                                                                      unit-cases of Coca-Cola to Beijing and
                                                                      Guangzhou. Coca-Cola was the first foreign
                                                                      consumer brand returned to the China market
                                                                      after the reform and opening up of China.
                                                                      1979 年 1 月，由中糧安排，3,000 箱可口可
                                                                      樂運抵北京和廣州，成為改革開放後第一個
                                                                      重返中國大陸市場的外國消費品牌。

                                                     MAJOR EVENTS OF CHINA FOODS IN 2018 中國食品 2018 年大事記




A MEETING BETWEEN COFCO AND THE COCA-COLA COMPANY TO
CELEBRATE THE 40TH ANNIVERSARY OF CHINA’S REFORM AND OPENING
UP AND THE RETURN OF COCA-COLA TO CHINA
中糧與可口可樂公司為紀念改革開放暨
可口可樂進入中國 40 年舉行見面會

On 27 November 2018, Chairman and Party Secretary
of COFCO, Mr. Lu Jun, met with Mr. James Quincey,
President and CEO of The Coca-Cola Company, and
The Coca-Cola Company’s global management team at
COFCO Fortune Plaza.
2018年11月27日，中糧黨組書記兼董事長呂軍先生
於中糧福臨門大廈會見了可口可樂公司總裁兼首席
執行官詹鯤傑先生及其全球管理團隊一行。




Both parties reviewed the achievements of COFCO and The Coca-Cola Company in China during the past four decades
and discussed new visions for future developments.
會談中，雙方共同回顧了四十年來中糧與可口可樂公司攜手在中國所取得的成就，並展望了未來發展的新思路。

                   YOU’RE ON THE TEAM –
      2018
                   WORLD CUP THEMED BRAND EVENT
                   有你才“隊”——世界杯主題品牌活動




     To ride on the World Cup fever, we launched a new packaging for bottles with the design
     of 31 national flags in our 2018 summer brand marketing campaign to cater for football-
     fanatic consumers. Offline roadshows were also held to further increase consumer
     engagement. A vivid World Cup theme at sales venues raised the customer preferences of
     Coca-Cola’s brand profile and promoted product consumption. As a result, market share
     effectively increased during the event.
     2018 年夏季品牌促銷借力世界杯，打造全民熱潮。31 款國旗手環瓶設計，讓志同道合
     的球迷夥伴共同為喜愛的球隊助威；線下路演沙龍與消費者狂歡互動；售點的世界杯
     主題生動化提升品牌喜好，拉動產品消費。活動期間，市場份額得到有效提升。

            REPRESENTATIVES OF THE COCA-COLA COMPANY VISITED
 2018
            CCBL’S MARKETS IN BEIJING
            可口可樂公司代表走訪中可飲料北京市場

                                                                                            On 27-28 November,
                                                                                            Mr. James Quincey,
                                                                                            President and CEO of
                                                                                            The Coca-Cola Company
                                                                                            and the company’s core
                                                                                            management team visited
                                                                                            CCBL’s market in Beijing.
                                                                                            Their activities included
                                                                                            examining new channels
                                                                                            such as online sales, new
retail supermarkets and convenience stores, as well as major sales channels such as general trade, tourism and schools.
The group’s visit led to a deep recognition of CCBL’s market execution and efforts to broaden its sales channels.
11 月 27-28 日，可口可樂公司全球總裁詹鯤傑先生及其核心管理團隊調研中可飲料北京市場，對電商渠道、新零售
超市、便利店等新興渠道和傳統渠道，旅遊及學校等重點渠道進行了走訪，對中可飲料的市場執行和新渠道的拓展
給與了充分的肯定和高度的認可。

                   CCBL WON SEVERAL AWARDS
      2018
                   AT THE COCA-COLA CHINA REGION CONTEST FOR 2017
                   中可飲料在可口可樂中國區2017年度獎項評比中榮獲多項大獎

     On 10 April, CCBL’s bottling plants won several awards
     in terms of the Coca-Cola China Region Contest for
     production quality and safety, sustainable development,
     point of sales performance, sales revenue growth and
     business execution.
     4 月 10 日，在可口可樂中國區獎項評選中，中可飲料
     裝瓶廠在生產質量安全、可持續發展、售點表現、銷
     售收入增長、商務執行力等獎項中均獲多項獎勵。




                   CCBL WON COCA-COLA GLOBAL CUSTOMER AND
      2018
                   COMMERCIAL LEADERSHIP AWARDS
                   中可飲料獲得可口可樂全球客戶及商務領導力大獎

                                                    On 16 May, CCBL’s PBMS project beat 122 outstanding entries from
                                                    32 countries and 14 bottling groups to win the 2017 GCCL (Global
                                                    Customer & Commercial Leadership) Awards. The occasion marked the
                                                    second time the honour had been bestowed on CCBL, after it won in the
                                                    “2016 Jiajiayue Project”.
                                                    5月16日，中可飲料的PBMS（合作夥伴業務管理系統）項目在全球14
                                                    個裝瓶集團，32 個國家，122 個優秀案例中脫穎而出，獲得可口可樂
                                                    2017 年全球 GCCL 大獎（The 2017 Global Customer and Commercial
                                                    Leadership Awards）。此獎項是繼中可飲料「2016 年家家悅項目」
                                                    後，再次獲此殊榮。

                                                         MAJOR EVENTS OF CHINA FOODS IN 2018 中國食品 2018 年大事記




            DIRECTORS OF THE COMPANY VISITED MAJOR MARKETS AND
2018
            PROVIDED GUIDANCE ON OPERATIONS
            中國食品董事多次走訪一線市場檢查指導




During 2018, members of the Company’s board of directors, including the existing Chairman of the board of directors,
had paid several visits to CCBL’s markets to receive work reports, inspect conditions and provide guidance on operations
and to bottling plants in Chongqing, Tianjin, Shaanxi, Sichuan, Shandong, Jilin and Gansu to promote a range of business
improvements.
2018 年，中國食品董事會成員包括現任董事會主席多次走訪中可飲料市場，聽取工作滙報，檢查市場，指導工作，

Based on statistics from the National Bureau of Statistics of the PRC,     根據中國國家統計局統計數據，2018 年中
China’s GDP achieved a year-on-year growth of 6.6% in 2018. The            國國內生產總值同比增長 6.6%。國民經濟

development and the growth and upgrading of consumer demand                費者需求的增長與升級，2018 年非酒精即

INTRODUCTION                                                            緒論
As a leading beverage company, China Foods Limited (hereinafter         中國食品有限公司（以下簡稱「中國食品」或
referred to as “China Foods” or the “Company”, together with COFCO      「本公司」，連同持有65%股權的中糧可口可
Coca-Cola Beverages Limited (“CCBL”), a 65%-owned subsidiary,           樂飲料有限公司（「中可飲料」及其附屬公司
and its subsidiaries, collectively as the “Group”) assumes the social   統稱「本集團」）作為一家行業內領先的飲料
responsibility philosophy of “loyalty to national policies and making   企業，傳承「忠於國計、良於民生」的社會責
people’s lives better”. It commits to the corporate mission and         任理念，致力於「奉獻時尚飲品，共創永恒
responsibility of “dedicating to modern beverages and creating long-    價值」的企業使命和責任，努力實現企業自
lasting value” and striveing to achieve sustainable development for     身和社會的可持續發展。
itself and society.


This document is the Company’s “Environmental, Social and               本報告乃本公司的《環境、社會及管治報告》
Governance Report” (hereinafter referred to as the “Report”). Its       （以下簡稱「本報告」）。本報告內容參照香港
contents were prepared in accordance with the Environmental,            聯合交易所有限公司證券上市規則（簡稱「上
Social and Governance Reporting Guide (“ESG Reporting Guide”)           市規則」）附錄二十七《環境、社會及管治報
in Appendix 27 of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on      告指引》
                                                                           （簡稱「ESG 指引」）而編製，並遵守
The Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”) and       了相關規定。本報告範圍涵蓋本公司在中國
are in compliance with all relevant regulations. The Report’s scope     的主要生產運營活動，報告期與 2018 財政
includes the Company’s major production and operational activities      年度一致。本報告旨在向社會各界披露本公
in China, and the reporting period is the 2018 financial year. The      司於2018年度在環境、社會及管治(ESG)領
purpose of the Report is to summarise the Company’s environmental,      域取得的工作成果，並應對各利益相關方之
social and governance (ESG) performance in 2018, and respond to         重點關切。
the concerns of various stakeholders.


The Company understands that the standard of its ESG management         本公司深刻理解我們在環境、社會及管治方
and performance is an important factor in achieving its strategic       面的管理水平和績效表現對實現本公司可持
sustainable development objectives. We hope that the Report will        續發展戰略目標的重要作用，我們希望通過
elicit constructive suggestions from the Company's stakeholders         本報告傾聽各利益相關方對本公司的良好建
to further enhance the Company’s management and performance             議，以此來進一步提升本公司在環境、社會
in this area. Please send your feedback on the Report by e-mail to      及管治相關領域的管理水平和績效表現。

                  at construction projects must be designed, built and                 識別施工和運營階段將產生的污

        In 2018, our plants provided feedback based on                   2018 年，本公司各工廠依據本
        the current regime of environmental inspection. The             次環保檢查反饋，結合環保部門
        Company subsequently rectified all environmental                要求、可口可樂公司及中糧審
        protection issues that were identified, according to the        核發現與環境評估，針對現場管
        requirements of environmental protection department             理、5S、現場 衛生等有 可能 影
        and combining the audit findings and environment                響環保達標或造成運營波動的各
        assessment of The Coca-Cola Company and COFCO.                  種風險，積極開展整改，確保各
        These focused on on-site management, 5S, on-site                工廠環保風險受控。
        health, and other risks with the potential to adversely
        affect environmental protections and/or stability of

                                                                                     企業管治報告


China Foods Limited (the “Company”) is committed to maintaining           中國食品有限公司（「本公司」）致力維持高
high standards of corporate governance. The board of directors of         水準的企業管治標準。本公司董事會（「董事
the Company (the “Board”) beliefs that good corporate governance          會」）相信良好的企業管治水平及常規對本集
standards and practices are crucial to improving the efficiency and       團之效能及表現以及維護股東、投資者、客
performance of the Group and to safeguarding the interests of our         戶及員工之利益致為重要。
shareholders, investors, customers and staff.


CORPORATE GOVERNANCE PRACTICES                                            企業管治常規
The Company’s corporate governance practices are based on                 本公司的企業管治常規乃根據香港聯合交易
the principles and code provisions (the “Code Provisions”) of the         所有限公司證券上市規則（「上市規則」
                                                                                           ）附錄
Corporate Governance Code (the “CG Code”) as set out in Appendix          十四《企業管治守則》
                                                                                   （「企業管治守則」）所載
14 of the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The Stock          的原則及守則條文（「守則條文」）為基準。
Exchange of Hong Kong Limited (the “Listing Rules”).


The Company has applied the principles of the CG Code and                 於 截 至 2018 年 12 月 31 日 止 年 度（「 本 年
complied with all the Code Provisions with the exception of Code          度」），本公司一直採用企業管治守則的原則
Provision E.1.2 throughout the year ended 31 December 2018 (the           並遵守守則條文，惟守則條文 E.1.2 除外。
“year”).


Under the Code Provision E.1.2 the chairman of the board should           根據守則條文 E.1.2，董事會主席應出席股
attend the annual general meeting. The former chairman of the             東周年大會。由於前董事會主席馬建平先生
Board, Mr. Ma Jianping, was unable to attend the annual general           因有其他要務未能出席本公司於2018年6月
meeting of the Company held on 1 June 2018 due to other                   1 日舉行的股東周年大會。本公司執行董事
engagement. Ms. Luan Xiuju, executive director and the managing           兼董事總經理欒秀菊女士根據本公司公司細
director of the Company, took the chair of the annual general             則（「公司細則」）的細則 73 條主持該股東周
meeting, in accordance with bye-law 73 of the bye-laws of the             年大會，而董事會的其他成員亦有出席該股
Company (the “Bye-Laws”), and other members of the Board were all         東周年大會。
present at that annual general meeting.

AND RELEVANT EMPLOYEES
The Company has adopted the Model Code for Securities                     本公司已採納上市規則附錄十所載《上市發
Transactions by Directors of Listed Issuers (the “Model Code”)            行人董事進行證券交易的標準守則》（「標準
contained in Appendix 10 to the Listing Rules as its own code of          守則」）作為本公司董事進行證券交易方面之
conduct regarding directors’ securities transactions. The Company         操守守則。本公司已向董事會各董事（「董
has made specific enquiry of all directors of the Board (“Director(s)”)   事」）就本年度內是否有任何未遵守標準守則
regarding any non-compliance with the Model Code during the year,         作出具體查詢，全體董事確認，彼等於本年

     SECURITIES TRANSACTIONS BY DIRECTORS                                      董事及有關僱員的證券交易（續）
     AND RELEVANT EMPLOYEES (continued)
     The Company has also adopted a code for securities transactions           本公司亦已就有關僱員進行本公司證券交易
     by relevant employees (the “Employees Trading Code”) based on             採納一套根據標準守則而編製的有關僱員進
     the Model Code concerning dealings by relevant employees in the           行證券交易守則（「僱員交易守則」）。可能擁

Board Diversity Policy                                                     董事會成員多元化政策
The Board has adopted a board diversity policy (available on the           於 2013 年 8 月董事 會已採納董事會成員多
Company’s Website) in August 2013 setting out the approach to              元化政策（於本公司網站可供查閱），當中
achieve diversity on the Board. The Board believes that a diversity        載有達致董事會多元化的方針。董事會認為
of perspectives can be achieved through consideration of a number          通過多個方面以考慮達致多元化的觀點，包
of factors, including but not limited to gender, age, cultural and         括（但不局限於）性別、年齡、文化及教育
educational background, work and professional experience, skills,          背景、工作及專業經驗、技能、知識及服務
knowledge and length of service. All appointment of the members            任期。所有董事會成員的委任均以用人唯才
of the Board will be based on meritocracy, and candidates will be          為原則，並在考慮人選時會按客觀標準並適
considered against objective criteria, having due regard for the           當考慮董事會成員多元化的裨益。最終將按
benefits of diversity of the Board. The ultimate decision will be based    人選的材幹及其可為董事會帶來提供的貢獻
on merits of the candidates and the contribution they will bring to        而作決定。本公司明白並深信董事會成員多
the Board. The Company recognises and embraces the benefits of             元化對提升本公司的表現素質裨益良多。提
having a diverse Board to enhance the quality of its performance.          名委員會肩負物色及提名具備合適資格可擔
The nomination committee has primary responsibility for identifying        任董事會成員的人選供董事會批准的主要職
and nominating, for approval by the Board, suitably qualified              責，並在履行此職責時，將充分考慮董事會
candidates to become members of the Board and, in carrying out this        成員多元化政策。
responsibility, will give adequate consideration to the board diversity
policy.


The Board will from time to time review the board diversity policy, as     董事會將在適當時候檢討董事會多元化政
appropriate, to ensure its effectiveness.                                  策，以確保其行之有效。

Opinion                                                                  意見
We have audited the consolidated financial statements of China           本核數師（以下簡稱「我們」）已審計列載於

referred to as the “Group”) set out on pages 130 to 240, which           公司」）及其附屬公司（以下統稱「貴集團」）
comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at 31       的綜合財務報表，此綜合財務報表包括於
December 2018, and the consolidated statement of profit or loss,         2018 年 12 月 31 日的綜合財務狀況表與截
consolidated statement of comprehensive income, consolidated             至該日止年度的綜合損益報表、綜合全面
statement of changes in equity and consolidated statement of             收入表、綜合權益變動表和綜合現金流量
cash flows for the year then ended, and notes to the consolidated        表，以及綜合財務報表附註，包括主要會
financial statements, including a summary of significant accounting      計政策概要。
policies.

In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and    我們認為，該等綜合財務報表已根據香港
fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31   會計師公會（「香港會計師公會」）頒佈的《香
December 2018, and of its consolidated financial performance and         港財務報告準則》 （「香港財務報告準則」  ）真
its consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance        實而公允地反映了 貴集團於 2018 年 12 月
with Hong Kong Financial Reporting Standards (“HKFRSs”)                  31 日的綜合財務狀況及 貴集團截至該日
issued by the Hong Kong Institute of Certified Public Accountants        止年度的綜合財務表現及綜合現金流量，
(“HKICPA”) and have been properly prepared in compliance                 並已遵照香港《公司條例》的披露規定妥為
with the disclosure requirements of the Hong Kong Companies              擬備。
Ordinance.

Basis for Opinion                                                        意見的基礎
We conducted our audit in accordance with Hong Kong Standards            我們已根據香港會計師公會頒佈的《香港審
on Auditing (“HKSAs”) issued by the HKICPA. Our responsibilities         計準則》進行審計。我們在該等準則下承擔
under those standards are further described in the Auditor’s             的責任已在本報告「核數師就審計綜合財務
Responsibilities for the Audit of the Consolidated Financial             報表承擔的責任」部分中作進一步闡述。
Statements section of our report. We are independent of the Group        根據香港會計師公會頒佈的《專業會計師
in accordance with the HKICPA’s Code of Ethics for Professional          道德守則》（以下簡稱（「守則」），我們獨立
Accountants (the “Code”), and we have fulfilled our other ethical        於 貴集團，並已履行守則中的其他專業
responsibilities in accordance with the Code. We believe that the        道德責任。我們相信，我們所獲得的審計
audit evidence we have obtained is sufficient and appropriate to         憑證能充足及適當地為我們的審計意見提
provide a basis for our opinion.                                         供基礎。

Key Audit Matters                                                        關鍵審計事項
Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional            關鍵審計事項是根據我們的專業判斷，認
judgment, were of most significance in our audit of the consolidated     為對本期綜合財務報表的審計最為重要的
financial statements of the current period. These matters were           事項。這些事項是在對綜合財務報表整體
addressed in the context of our audit of the consolidated financial      進行審計並形成意見的背景下進行處理
statements as a whole, and in forming our opinion thereon, and we        的，我們不對這些事項提供單獨的意見。
do not provide a separate opinion on these matters.

      1. CORPORATE INFORMATION                                                   1. 公司資料
           China Foods Limited (the “Company”) is a limited liability              中 國 食 品 有 限 公 司（「 本 公 司 」）為 一
           company incorporated in Bermuda. The registered office of               家於百慕達註冊成立之有限責任公
           the Company is located at Clarendon House, 2 Church Street,             司。本公司之註冊辦事處為 Clarendon
           Hamilton HM 11, Bermuda.                                                House, 2 Church Street, Hamilton HM
                                                                                   11, Bermuda。

           The Company is a subsidiary of China Foods (Holdings)                   本公司乃在英屬維爾京群島註冊成立
           Limited, a company incorporated in the British Virgin                   之中國食品（控股）有限公司之附屬公
           Islands. In the opinion of the directors of the Company                 司。 本 公 司 董 事（「 董 事 」）認 為， 最
           (the “Directors”), the ultimate holding company is COFCO                終控股公司為於中華人民共和國（「中
           Corporation, which is a state-owned enterprise registered in the        國」）註冊的國有企業中糧集團有限公
           Peoples Republic of China (the “PRC”).                                  司。

           During the year, the Company and its subsidiaries (collectively        於本年度，本公司及其附屬公司（統稱
           referred to as the “Group”) were involved in processing,               「本集團」）參與加工、裝瓶及分銷汽水
           bottling and distribution of sparkling beverage products, and           飲料產品以及分銷不含氣飲料產品。
           distribution of still beverage products.

           The primary economic environment for the Group entities is              本集團實體的主要經營環境在中國內
           Mainland China. It is Renminbi (“RMB”) that mostly influences           地。人民幣（「人民幣」）是主要影響本
           the performance of the Group entities and the underlying                集團實體的表現與本集團實體的相關
           transactions, events and conditions relevant to the Group               交易、事件及狀況的貨幣。因此，本
           entities. Accordingly, the functional currency of the Company           公司功能貨幣已由港元（「港元」）追溯
           has been changed from Hong Kong Dollar (“HKD”) into RMB                 變更為人民幣。簡明綜合財務報表的

           current year. Comparative figures have been re-presented in

      2. Basis of preparation                                                    2. 編製基準
           The consolidated financial statements have been prepared                儘管本集團於報告期末有流動負債淨
           on a going concern basis notwithstanding that the Group had             額人民幣 2,467,940,000 元，本財務資

           reporting period. In preparing these consolidated financial             等綜合財務報表時，董事已審慎考慮
           statements, the Directors have given careful consideration              本集團目前及預期未來的流動資金。
           to the current and anticipated future liquidity of the Group.           經考慮（其中包括）( i ) 於報告期末尚未
           Taking into account, inter alia, (i) the unutilised loan facilities     使用的貸款授信；及 ( i i ) 本集團未來

           cash inflows generated from the Group’s operations for the              現金流入淨額。董事認為，本集團將
           next twelve months. The Directors are of the opinion that the           能夠應付其到期負債。因此，董事認
           Group will be able to meet its liabilities as and when they fall        為按持續經營基準編製此等綜合財務
           due. Accordingly, the Directors consider that the preparation           報表乃屬適當。

4.	SIGNIFICANT ACCOUNTING POLICIES                                         4. 主要會計政策（續）
    (continued)

    Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset          公平值為市場於計量日在有序交易中
    or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between        出售資產所收取的價格或轉讓的負債
    market participants at the measurement date, regardless of               所支付的價格，不論該價格是否可直
    whether that price is directly observable or estimated using             接觀察或以其他估值技巧估計。在估
    another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value               計資產或負債之過程中，本集團已計
    of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the            算該資產或負債之特性，倘若市場參
    characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants        與人將考慮該特性而於計量日對該資
    would take those characteristics into account when pricing              產或負債出價。於綜合財務報表作為
    the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for          計量及╱或披露公平值均按此基準而
    measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated            釐定，包含在香港財務報告準則第2號
    financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for          「以股代款」範圍內之以股份為基礎支
    share-based payment transactions that are within the scope of           付之交易，包含在香港會計準則第 17
    HKFRS 2 Share-based Payment, leasing transactions that are              號範圍內之租賃交易，及若干類似公
    within the scope of HKAS 17 Leases, and measurements that               平值但並非公平值之計量，如香港會
    have some similarities to fair value but are not fair value, such       計準則第2號存貨可變現淨值或香港會
    as net realisable value in HKAS 2 Inventories or value in use in        計準則第 36 號資產之減值使用價值等
    HKAS 36 Impairment of Assets.                                           則除外。

    A fair value measurement of a non-financial asset takes into             非金融資產之公平值計量計及市場參
    account a market participant’s ability to generate economic              與者通過使用其資產之最高及最佳用
    benefits by using the asset in its highest and best use or by            途或將其出售予將使用其最高及最佳
    selling it to another market participant that would use the asset        用途之另一市場參與者，而能夠產生
    in its highest and best use.                                             經濟利益之能力。

    For financial instruments and investment properties which                就按公平值交易的金融工具及投資物
    are transacted at fair value and a valuation technique that              業以及於其後期間使用不可觀察輸入
    unobservable inputs is to be used to measure fair value in               數據計量公平值的估值技術而言，估
    subsequent periods, the valuation technique is calibrated so             值技術會進行調整，以致初步確認時
    that at initial recognition the results of the valuation technique       估值技術的結果等於交易價格。
    equals the transaction price.

    In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value                此外，作為財務呈報用途，用於計量
    measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on               公平值的輸入數據的可觀察程度和重
    the degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements            要性來決定的公平值架構級別分類（第
    are observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair            一、第二及第三級別），陳述如下：
    value measurement in its entirety, which are described as
    follows:

    •    Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active             •   第一級別輸入數據為於活躍市場
         markets for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can         之標價（未經調整），以識別資產
         access at the measurement date;                                         或負債，而該實體於計量日可作
                                                                                 評估；

    •    Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted prices                 •   第二級別輸入數據為除了第一級
         included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset              別所含標價之外直接地或間接地
         or liability, either directly or indirectly; and                        可觀察之資產或負債；及

    •    Level 3 inputs are unobservable inputs for the asset or             •   第三級別輸入數據為資產或負債
         liability.                                                              的不可觀察之輸入數據。

           The principal accounting policies are set out below.                           所採用主要會計政策如下。

           Basis of Consolidation                                                         合併基準
           The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial                本綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公
           statements of the Company and entities controlled by the                       司及其附屬公司所控制企業。當本公
           Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the                     司符合以下要素時，則取得控制權：
           Company:

           •     has power over the investee;                                             •    可對投資對象行使權力；

           •     is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its                  •    因參與被投資對象而就浮動回報
                 involvement with the investee; and                                            承擔風險或享有權利；及

           •     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.                  •    有能力行使其權力以影響其回
                                                                                               報。

           The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if                 倘有事實及情況顯示上文所述三個控
           facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one                 制元素其中一項或超過一項之改變，
           or more of the three elements of control listed above.                         本集團重新評估是否已控制投資對象。

           Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains                    當本集團獲得一附屬公司之控制權開
           control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses                    始綜合該附屬公司，而當本集團失去
           control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses                   該附屬公司之控制權時結束綜合。尤
           of a subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are                    其是年內所購入或出售附屬公司之收
           included in the consolidated statement of profit or loss from                  入及支出由本集團獲得控制權當日起
           the date the Group gains control until the date when the Group                 包括在綜合損益表直至本集團停止該
           ceases to control the subsidiary.                                              附屬公司之控制權日期為止。

           Profit or loss and each item of other comprehensive income                     損益及其他全面收益之每個項目均歸
           are attributed to the owners of the Company and to the                         屬於本公司之擁有人及非控制性權
           non-controlling interests. Total comprehensive income of                       益。該等附屬公司之總全面收益歸屬
           subsidiaries is attributed to the owners of the Company and                    於本公司之擁有人及非控制性權益，
           to the non-controlling interests even if this results in the non-              即使導致非控制性權益之業績為赤字
           controlling interests having a deficit balance.                                結餘。

           All intragroup assets and liabilities, equity, income, expenses                凡本集團成員公司之間的交易所產生
           and cash flows relating to transactions between members of                     的集團間資產及負債、權益、收入、
           the Group are eliminated in full on consolidation.                             支出及現金流均於合併時全額抵銷。

           Non-controlling interests in subsidiaries are presented                        於附屬公司之非控股權益與本集團於
           separately from the Group’s equity therein, which represent                    當中的權益分開呈列，指現時擁有之
           present ownership interests entitling their holders to a                       權益且賦予持有人權利於清盤時按比
           proportionate share of net assets of the relevant subsidiaries                 例分佔相關附屬公司淨資產。
           upon liquidation.

    acquiree. Acquisition-related costs are generally recognised in

    measured in accordance with HKAS 12 Income Taxes.                      量之遞延稅項資產或負債除外。

    Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently             所得稅開支指現時應付稅項與遞延稅

    year. Taxable profit differs from profit before tax, because of        算。因收入或開支為於其他年度應課
    income or expense that are taxable or deductible in other years        稅或可扣稅之收入或開支項目，及毋

    been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of the                期稅項負債乃按呈報期結算日已頒佈
    reporting period.                                                      或已實質頒佈的稅率計算。

    liabilities are generally recognised for all taxable temporary         遞延所得稅負債均予確認。遞延所得
    differences. Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for          稅資產只在很可能取得能利用該可抵
    all deductible temporary differences to the extent that it is          扣暫時性差異來抵扣的應納稅所得額
    probable that taxable profits will be available against which          的限度內予以確認一般所有應課稅
    those deductible temporary differences can be utilised. Such           暫。若於一項交易中，商譽或初步確

    from the initial recognition of goodwill.

           that are expected to apply in the period in which the liability is           日已頒佈或實質頒佈的稅率（及稅法）

           that have been enacted or substantively enacted by the end of                用稅率計量。
           the reporting period.

           which the Group expects, at the end of the reporting period, to              產及負債賬面值的方式產生的稅務影
           recover or settle the carrying amount of its assets and liabilities.         響。

           For the purposes of measuring deferred tax for investment                    就計量遞延稅項而言，利用公平值模

           carrying amounts of such properties are presumed to be                       過銷售全數收回。
           recovered entirely through sale.

           legally enforceable right to set off current tax assets against              得稅資產的法定權利，且遞延所得稅

           levied by the same taxation authority and the Group intends to               所得稅相關，且本集團擬以淨額為基

                                                                                        相互抵銷。

           Current and deferred tax are recognised in profit or loss. Where             即期及遞延稅項於損益確認。倘即期

    identified.                                                            單元組合。

    Recoverable amount is the higher of fair value less costs              可收回金額是指公平值減去銷售成本
    of disposal and value in use. In assessing value in use, the           後的餘額和使用價值兩者中的較高
    estimated future cash flows are discounted to their present            者。在評估使用價值時，預計未來現
    value using a pre-tax discount rate that reflects current market       金流量會採用稅前折現率折現為現

           Inventories                                                                  存貨
           Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable               存貨是以存貨成本和可變現淨值兩者
           value. Costs of inventories are determined on a weighted                     中的較低者列賬。存貨成本採用加權
           average method. Net realisable value represents the estimated                平均法計量。可變現淨值是以日常業
           selling price for inventories less all estimated costs of                    務過程中的估計售價減去完成生產及
           completion and costs necessary to make the sale.                             銷售所需的估計成本後所得數額。

           Financial Instruments                                                        金融工具
           Financial assets and financial liabilities are recognised when a             金融資產和金融負債當實體成為金融
           group entity becomes a party to the contractual provisions of                工具合同條款的訂約方時予以確定。
           the instrument. All regular way purchases or sales of financial              所有常規方式購買或出售的金融資產
           assets are recognised and derecognised on a trade date basis.                應以交易日為基礎進行確認和終止確
           Regular way purchases or sales are purchases or sales of                     認。常規方式購買或出售是指按照法
           financial assets that require delivery of assets within the time             規或市場慣例所確立的時間安排來交
           frame established by regulation or convention in the market                  付資產的金融資產的購買或出售。
           place.

           Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured            除與客戶簽訂的合同產生的貿易應收
           at fair value except for trade receivables arising from contracts            款項自2018年1月1日起開始按照香港
           with customers which are initially measured in accordance                    財務報告準則第 15 條進行計量外，金
           with HKFRS 15 since 1 January 2018. Transaction costs that                   融資產和金融負債以公平值進行初始
           are directly attributable to the acquisition or issue of financial           計量。被歸入收購或發行金融資產和
           assets and financial liabilities are added to or deducted from               金融負債的直接應佔的交易費用於初
           the fair value of the financial assets or financial liabilities, as          始確認時計入金融資產或金融負債的
           appropriate, on initial recognition.                                         公平值，或從中扣除。

           The effective interest method is a method of calculating the                實際利率法是計算金融資產或金融負
           amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and              債攤餘成本以及在相關期間內分配利
           of allocating interest income and interest expense over the                  息收支的一種方法。實際利率是指將
           relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate that                金融工具在預計存續期或更短期間內
           exactly discounts estimated future cash receipts and payments               （如適當）的估計未來現金收款額（包括
           (including all fees and points paid or received that form an                支付或收取的構成實際利率組成部分
           integral part of the effective interest rate, transaction costs and         的所有費用和貼息、交易費用以及其
           other premiums or discounts) through the expected life of the               他溢價或折價）折現為該工具初始確認
           financial asset or financial liability, or, where appropriate, a            時賬面淨額所使用的利率。
           shorter period, to the net carrying amount on initial recognition.

           Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all                   存續期預期信用損失指相關工具的預
           possible default events over the expected life of the relevant              期使用期內所有可能的違約事件產生
           instrument. In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents                之預期信用損失。相反，12 個月預期
           the portion of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from                 信用損失（「12 個月預期信用損失」）指
           default events that are possible within 12 months after the                 預期於報告日期後 12 個月內可能發生
           reporting date. Assessment are done based on the Group’s                    的違約事件導致之存續期預期信用損
           historical credit loss experience, adjusted for factors that are            失部分。評估乃根據本集團的歷史信
           specific to the debtors, general economic conditions and an                 用損失經驗進行，並根據債務人特有
           assessment of both the current conditions at the reporting date             的因素、一般經濟狀況以及對報告日
           as well as the forecast of future conditions.                               期當前狀況的評估以及對未來狀況的
                                                                                       預測作出調整。


           The Group always recognises lifetime ECL for accounts                       本集團通常就應收賬款確認存續期預
           receivables. The ECL on these assets are assessed collectively              期信用損失。該等資產的預期信用損
           using a provision matrix with appropriate groupings.                        失將使用適當組別的撥備矩陣進行集
                                                                                       體評估。

           For all other instruments, the Group measures the loss                      對於所有其他工具，本集團計量的虧
           allowance equal to 12m ECL, unless when there has been a                    損準備等於 12 個月預期信用損失，除
           significant increase in credit risk since initial recognition, the          非自初始確認後信用風險顯著增加，
           Group recognises lifetime ECL. The assessment of whether                    則本集團確認存續期預期信用損失。
           lifetime ECL should be recognised is based on significant                   是否應確認存續期預期信用損失的評
           increases in the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since            估乃基於自初始確認以來發生違約之
           initial recognition.                                                        可能性或風險的顯著增加而定。

           Significant increase in credit risk                                         信貸風險大幅增加
           In assessing whether the credit risk has increased significantly            於評估自初始確認後信用風險是否顯
           since initial recognition, the Group compares the risk of a                 著增加時，本集團將於報告日期金融
           default occurring on the financial instrument as at the reporting           工具發生之違約風險與初始確認日起
           date with the risk of a default occurring on the financial                  金融工具發生之違約風險進行比較。
           instrument as at the date of initial recognition. In making                 在進行該評估時，本集團會考慮合理
           this assessment, the Group considers both quantitative and                  且可支持的定量和定性資料，包括無
           qualitative information that is reasonable and supportable,                 需付出不必要的成本或努力而可得之
           including historical experience and forward-looking information             歷史經驗及前瞻性資料。
           that is available without undue cost or effort.

           Definition of default                                                          違約定義
           For internal credit risk management, the Group considers an                    就內部信貸風險管理而言，本集團認
           event of default occurs when information developed internally                  為，違約事件在內部制訂或得自外界
           or obtained from external sources indicates that the debtor is                 來源的資料顯示債務人不大可能悉數
           unlikely to pay its creditors, including the Group, in full (without           向債權人（包括本集團）還款（未計及本
           taking into account any collaterals held by the Group).                        集團所持任何抵押品）時發生。

           Credit-impaired financial assets                                               信用減值金融資產
           A financial asset is credit-impaired when one or more events of                金融資產在一項或以上違約事件（對該
           default that have a detrimental impact on the estimated future                 金融資產估計未來現金流量構成不利
           cash flows of that financial asset have occurred. Evidence that                影響）發生時維持信用減值。金融資產
           a financial asset is credit impaired includes observable data                  維持信用減值的證據包括有關下列事
           about the following events:                                                    件的可觀察數據：

           •     significant financial difficulty of the issuer or the borrower;          •    發行人或借款人的重大財困；

           •     a breach of contract, such as a default or past due event;               •    違反合約（如違約或逾期事件）；

           •     the lender(s) of the borrower, for economic or contractual               •    借款人的貸款人因有關借款人財
                 reasons relating to the borrower’s financial difficulty,                      困的經濟或合約理由而向借款
                 having granted to the borrower a concession(s) that the                       人批出貸款人不會另行考慮的優
                 lender(s) would not otherwise consider;                                       惠；

           •     it is becoming probable that the borrower will enter                     •    借款人將可能陷入破產或其他財
                 bankruptcy or other financial reorganisation; or                              務重組；或

           •     the disappearance of an active market for that financial                 •    因財務困難導致金融資產無法在
                 asset because of financial difficulties.                                      活躍市場繼續交易。

           Write-off policy                                                               撇銷政策
           The Group writes off a financial asset when there is information               資料顯示對手方處於嚴重財困及無實
           indicating that the counterparty is in severe financial difficulty             際收回可能時（例如對手方被清盤或已
           and there is no realistic prospect of recovery, for example,                   進入破產程序時），本集團則撇銷金融
           when the counterparty has been placed under liquidation or                     資產。經考慮法律意見後（倘合適），
           has entered into bankruptcy proceedings. Financial assets                      遭撇銷的金融資產可能仍須按本集團
           written off may still be subject to enforcement activities                     收回程序進行強制執行活動。撇銷構
           under the Group’s recovery procedures, taking into account                     成取消確認事項。任何其後收回在損
           legal advice where appropriate. A write-off constitutes a                      益中確認。
           derecognition event. Any subsequent recoveries are recognised
           in profit or loss.

    Measurement and recognition of ECL                                    預期信貸損失之計量及確認
    The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability of            預期信用損失之計量為違約概率、違
    default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the loss if        約虧損（即違約時虧損大小）及違約時
    there is a default) and the exposure at default. The assessment       風險敞口之函數。違約概率及違約虧
    of the probability of default and loss given default is based         損之評估乃基於歷史數據按前瞻性資
    on historical data adjusted by forward-looking information.           料作調整。預期信用損失的預估乃無
    Estimation of ECL effects on unbiased and probability-weighted        偏概率加權平均金額，以各自發生違
    amount that is determined with the respective risks of default        約的風險為權重確定。
    occurring as the weights.

    Generally, the ECL is estimated as the difference between all          一般而言，預期信用損失為根據合約
    contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in receivable,        應付本集團之所有合約現金流量與本
    the cash accordance with the contract and all the cash flows           集團預期收取之所有現金流量之間的
    that the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective         差額（按初始確認時釐定之有效利率貼
    interest rate determined at initial recognition.                       現）。

    Where ECL is measured on a collective basis or cater for cases         倘預期信用損失按集體基準計量或迎
    where evidence at the individual instrument level may not yet          合個別工具水平證據未必存在的情
    be available, the financial instruments are grouped on the             況，則金融工具按以下基準歸類：
    basis:

    •    Ageing;                                                           •   賬齡；

    •    Nature, size and industry of debtors; and                         •   債務人的性質、規模及行業；及

    •    External credit ratings where available.                          •   外部信貸評級（倘有）。

    The grouping is regularly reviewed by management to ensure             歸類工作經管理層定期檢討，以確保
    the constituents of each group continue to share similar credit        各組別成份繼續分擔類似信貸風險特
    risk characteristics.                                                  性。

    Interest income is calculated based on the gross carrying              利息收入根據金融資產的賬面總額計
    amount of the financial asset unless the financial asset is credit     算，但金融資產發生信用減值的情況
    impaired, in which case interest income is calculated based on         除外，此時利息收入根據金融資產的
    amortised cost of the financial asset.                                 攤餘成本計算。

    The Group recognises an impairment gain or loss in profit              本集團通過調整損失撥備賬戶於損益
    or loss for all financial instruments with the corresponding           中確認所有金融工具的減值損益。
    adjustment is recognised through a loss allowance account.

           On 21 March 2018, the Hong Kong Legislative Council passed                 於 2018 年 3 月 21 日，香港立法會通過
           The Inland Revenue (Amendment) (No. 7) Bill 2017 (the                     《2017 年稅務（修訂）     （第 7 號）條例草
           “Bill”) which introduces the two-tiered profits tax rates regime.          案》（「條例草案」）引入利得稅兩級制。
           The Bill was signed into law on 28 March 2018 and was                      該條例草案於 2018 年 3 月 28 日簽署成
           gazetted on the following day. Under the two-tiered profits tax           為法律，並於次日刊登憲報。根據利
           rates regime, the first HKD2 million of profits of the qualifying         得稅兩級制，合資格集團實體首2百萬
           group entity will be taxed at 8.25%, and profits above HKD2               港元溢利將按 8.25% 的稅率繳稅，超
           million will be taxed at 16.5%. The profits of group entities             過2百萬港元的溢利將按16.5%的稅率
           not qualifying for the two-tiered profits tax rates regime will           繳稅。不符合兩級制利得稅資格的集
           continue to be taxed at a flat rate of 16.5%.                             團實體的溢利將繼續按 16.5% 之劃一
                                                                                     稅率徵收稅項。

           The Directors considered the amount involved upon                          董事認為，實施利得稅兩級制所涉及
           implementation of the two-tiered profits tax rates regime                  之金額與綜合財務報表並無重大關
           as insignificant to the consolidated financial statements.                 係。因此，自本年度起，首2百萬港元
           Accordingly, starting from the current year, the Hong Kong                 之估計應課稅溢利按 8.25% 計算香港
           profits tax is calculated at 8.25% on the first HKD2 million               利得稅，而2百萬港元以上之估計應課
           of the estimated assessable profits and at 16.5% on the                    稅溢利則按 16.5% 計算香港利得稅。
           estimated assessable profits above HKD2 million.

           Under the Law of the People’s Republic of China on Enterprise              根據中國企業所得稅法（「企業所得稅
           Income Tax (the “EIT Law”) and Implementation Regulation                   法」）及企業所得稅法實施條例，本集
           of the EIT Law, the tax rate of the PRC subsidiaries is 25% for            團中國附屬公司稅率兩年均為 25%。
           both years.

      40. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                              40. 資本風險管理
           The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the             本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團的
           Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                  實體能夠持續經營，同時透過優化債
           maximising the return to stakeholders through the optimisation           務及股權平衡最大限度地提高股東回
           of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy             報。自去年起本集團整體策略保持不
           remains unchanged from prior year.                                       變。

           The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt, which           本集團資本架構包括債務淨額，其中
           includes bank borrowings disclosed in note 28, net of bank               包括附註 28 所披露銀行借款（已扣減
           balances and cash and equity attributable to owners of the               銀行結餘及現金）及本公司擁有人應佔
           Company, comprising issued share capital, share premium                  權益（包括已發行股本、股份溢價及儲
           and reserves.                                                            備）。

           The management reviews the capital structure on an annual                管理層按年審閱資本架構。作為該審
           basis. As part of this review, the management considers the              閱的一部分，管理層將考慮資本成本
           cost of capital and the risks associated with each class of              及與各類別資本相關的風險。
           capital.

    Interest rate risk                                                 利率風險
    The Group is exposed to cash flow interest rate risk in            本集團承受與浮息銀行結餘及浮息銀
    relation to variable rate bank balances and variable-rate bank     行借款相關之現金流量利率風險。本
    borrowings. The Group cash flow interest rate risk is mainly       集團現金流量利率風險主要集中於銀
    concentrated on the fluctuation of interest rates on bank          行結餘及銀行借款利率波動。本集團
    balances and bank borrowings. The Group aims at keeping            旨在以浮動利率維持借款。本集團通
    borrowings at variable rates. The Group manages its interest       過評估利率水平及前景所產生的任何
    rate exposures by assessing the potential impact arising from      利率變動所產生的潛在影響管理其利
    any interest rate movements based on interest rate level and       率風險。
    outlook.
